ABOUT HAICHANG OCEAN PARK HOLDINGS LTD.
(THE “COMPANY” OR “HAICHANG” OR “HAICHANG OCEAN PARK”,
TOGETHER WITH ITS SUBSIDIARIES, THE “GROUP”)
有關海昌海洋公園控股有限公司
（「本公司」或「海昌」或「海昌海洋公園」，連同其附屬公司統稱「本集團」）

parks and one theme park under construction, and authorised            家以海洋文化為核心的主題公園和一家
four theme parks to use the brand of “Haichang”. We offer visitors     在建主題公園，同時授權許可4個主題公
a comprehensive tourism experience encompassing leisure,               園使用海昌品牌。通過主題公園與配套
entertainment, dining, shopping and accommodation in our theme         商業設施，我們為遊客提供集休閒、娛
parks together with auxiliary commercial facilities. Meanwhile, we     樂、餐飲、購物、住宿於一體的綜合性

and capability across the country, so that more consumers can          海洋文化與海洋能力輸出到全國各地，

turnover of approximately RMB793.0 million (2021: approximately           錄得營業額約人民幣793.0百萬元（2021
RMB2,460.2 million), representing a decrease of approximately             年 ： 約 人 民 幣 2 , 4 6 0 . 2 百 萬 元 ）， 較 上
67.8% when compared with last year. In 2022, as affected by the           年度降低約67.8%。2022 年，由於受
resurgence of the COVID-19 pandemic, the Group has complied               COVID-19疫情反覆影響，本集團遵循各
with the prevention and control guidance and requirements                 地相關部門的防控指引和要求（如階段性
imposed by relevant local authorities, such as phased closures            閉園、限流等），以及部份公園已於2021
of the parks and crowd restrictions, which, together with the             年底剝離，對我們的公園運營業務產生
disposal of certain parks in late 2021, materially affected our park      了較大的影響。

     In order to supplement the Group’s consolidated financial               為補充本集團按照國際財務報告準則呈
     statements, which are presented in accordance with IFRSs, the           列的合併財務報表，本集團將經調整
     Group uses adjusted EBITDA (loss)/profit and adjusted net loss as       E B I T D A（虧損）╱ 利潤及經調整淨虧損
     additional financial measures. The Group presents these financial       作為額外財務計量。本集團呈列該等財
     measures because they are used by the Group’s management                務計量乃由於本集團管理層使用該等財
     to evaluate the Group’s financial performance by eliminating            務計量，透過撇除於報告期本集團認為
     the impact of certain items that the Group does not consider to         對本集團表現不具指示性的若干項目的
     be indicative of the Group’s performance during the reporting           影響以評估本集團的財務表現。本集團
     period. The Group also believes that these non-IFRSs measures           亦認為此非國際財務報告準則計量為投
     provide additional information to investors and others in their         資者及其他人士提供額外資料，有助於
     understanding and evaluating the Group’s results of operations in       彼等與本集團管理層採用同樣方式理解
     the same manner as they help the Group’s management. These              並評估本集團經營業績。該非國際財務
     non-IFRSs measures provide an unbiased presentation for investors       報告準則為投資者提供公正的陳述以理
     for understanding. However, these non-IFRSs measures do not             解。然而此非國際財務報告準則計量並
     have the standardized meanings prescribed by IFRSs and therefore        未具有國際財務報告準則規定的標準化
     they may not be comparable to similar measures presented by             涵義，因此可能與其他在聯交所上市的
     other companies listed on the Stock Exchange.                           公司所呈列的類似措施不具可比性。

     As indicated by the above figures, the Group has maintained             上述數字顯示，本集團擁有穩健財務資
     stable financial resources to meet its future commitments and           源，可應付其未來承擔及未來投資以進

CORPORATE GOVERNANCE PRACTICES                                    企業管治常規
The Group is committed to maintaining high standards of           本集團致力維持高水平的企業管治，以
corporate governance to safeguard the interests of shareholders   保障股東利益及提升企業價值和問責

The Group does not tolerate any form of bribery, whether direct         本公司絕不容忍其董事、高級職員、僱

give the appearance of being involved in corruption or unethical        牽涉入賄賂及不道德商業行為之情況，
business conduct, so as to avoid such conduct which is clearly          從而避免作出該等屬明確禁止之行為，
prohibited, and to promptly seek guidance where necessary.              並於必要時迅速尋求指引。

To the shareholders of Haichang Ocean Park Holdings Ltd.                致海昌海洋公園控股有限公司各股東
(Incorporated in the Cayman Islands with limited liability)            （於開曼群島註冊成立之有限公司）

 Key audit matter                                             How our audit addressed the key audit matter
 關鍵審核事項                                                       關鍵審核事項在審核中的處理方法

 Based on the outcome of the impairment tests, the
 Group recognised an impairment of RMB146,373,000
 in profit or loss for the year ended 31 December
 2022. The impairment testing was important to our
 audit due to the magnitude of the carrying amount of
 the assets for operation (65.06% of the total assets
 as at 31 December 2022) and the judgement involved
 in determining key assumptions such as future
 sales growth, profit margins and discount rates, in
 particular for theme parks with an aggregate carrying
 amount of RMB4,855,687,000 as at 31 December
 2022 which incurred losses.


 基於減值測試的結果， 貴集團在截至2022 年12 月
 31 日止年度的損益中確認減值人民幣146,373,000
 元。鑒於營運資產賬面值龐大（於2022年12月31日
 佔總資產的65.06%）及在釐定未來銷售額增長、利
 潤率及折現率等主要假設過程中所涉及的判斷，該
 減值測試對吾等的審核工作意義重大，尤其是，於
 2022年12月31日賬面總值為人民幣4,855,687,000
 元的主題公園錄得虧損。

 Relevant disclosures are included in notes 4 and 12
 to the consolidated financial statements.


 相關披露載於合併財務報表附註4及12。

on the basis of these consolidated financial statements.               大錯誤陳述。

           2022. A subsidiary is an entity (including a structured entity),        公司為本公司直接或間接控制的實
           directly or indirectly, controlled by the Company. Control              體（包括結構性實體）。當本集團對
           is achieved when the Group is exposed, or has rights, to                參與投資對象業務的浮動回報承擔

           has the ability to affect those returns through its power over          對象的權力（即本集團獲賦予現有能
           the investee (i.e., existing rights that give the Group the             力以主導投資對象相關活動的既存
           current ability to direct the relevant activities of the investee).     權利）影響該等回報時，即取得控制
                                                                                   權。

           indirectly, less than a majority of the voting or similar rights        或間接擁有少於投資對象大多數投

           circumstances in assessing whether it has power over an                 評估其是否擁有對投資對象的權力
           investee, including:                                                    時會考慮一切相關事實及情況，包

3.   SUMMARY OF SIGNIFICANT                                              3. 重大會計政策概要
     ACCOUNTING POLICIES
     Investment in an associate                                              於聯營公司的投資
     An associate is an entity in which the Group has a long-term            聯營公司為本集團擁有股本表決權
     interest of generally not less than 20% of the equity voting            整體不少於20%的長期權益且可行
     rights and over which it is in a position to exercise significant       使重大影響力的實體。重大影響力
     influence. Significant influence is the power to participate in         為參與投資對象財務及經營政策決
     the financial and operating policy decisions of the investee,           定的權力，但並非該等政策的控制
     but is not control or joint control over those policies.                權或共同控制權。

     The Group’s investment in an associate is stated in the                 本集團於聯營公司的投資根據權益
     consolidated statement of financial position at the Group’s             會計法，按本集團分佔的淨資產減
     share of net assets under the equity method of accounting,              任何減值虧損，於合併財務狀況表
     less any impairment losses.                                             中列賬。

     The Group’s share of the post-acquisition results and other             本集團分佔聯營公司的收購後業績
     comprehensive income of the associate is included in the                及其他全面收益，分別計入合併損
     consolidated statement of profit or loss and consolidated               益及合併其他全面收益表內。此
     other comprehensive income, respectively. In addition, when             外，如有直接確認於聯營公司權益
     there has been a change recognised directly in the equity               的變動，本集團於適當情況下在合
     of the associate, the Group recognises its share of any                 併權益變動表中確認其分佔的任何
     changes, when applicable, in the consolidated statement                 變動。本集團與其聯營公司之間的
     of changes in equity. Unrealised gains and losses resulting             交易所產生的未變現收益及虧損予
     from transactions between the Group and its associate are               以對銷，以本集團於該聯營公司的
     eliminated to the extent of the Group’s investment in the               投資為限，惟倘未變現虧損證明所
     associate, except where unrealised losses provide evidence              轉讓資產出現減值則除外。由收購
     of an impairment of the assets transferred. Goodwill arising            聯營公司產生的商譽計入為本集團
     from the acquisition of the associate is included as part of            於聯營公司的投資一部分。
     the Group’s investment in the associate.


     When an investment in an associate is classified as held for            倘於聯營公司的投資分類為持作出
     sale, it is accounted for in accordance with IFRS 5 Non-                售，則根據國際財務報告準則第5號
     current Assets Held for Sale and Discontinued Operations .              持作出售的非流動資產及已終止經
                                                                             營業務 入賬。

           Fair value measurement                                              公允價值計量
           The Group measures its investment properties and equity             本集團於各報告期末按公允價值計
           investments at fair value at the end of each reporting period.      量其投資物業及股本投資。公允價
           Fair value is the price that would be received to sell an           值為市場參與者於計量日期在有序
           asset or paid to transfer a liability in an orderly transaction     交易中出售資產所收取的價格或轉
           between market participants at the measurement date.                讓負債所支付的價格。公允價值計
           The fair value measurement is based on the presumption              量乃根據假設出售資產或轉讓負債
           that the transaction to sell the asset or transfer the liability    的交易於資產或負債主要市場或（在
           takes place either in the principal market for the asset or         無主要市場情況下）最具優勢市場進
           liability, or in the absence of a principal market, in the most     行而作出。主要或最具優勢市場須
           advantageous market for the asset or liability. The principal       為本集團可進入之市場。資產或負
           or the most advantageous market must be accessible by the           債的公允價值乃按假設市場參與者
           Group. The fair value of an asset or a liability is measured        於資產或負債定價時會以其最佳經
           using the assumptions that market participants would use            濟利益行事計量。
           when pricing the asset or liability, assuming that market
           participants act in their economic best interest.

     Fair value measurement (continued)                                       公允價值計量（續）
     A fair value measurement of a non-financial asset takes into             非金融資產的公允價值計量須計及
     account a market participant’s ability to generate economic              市場參與者能自最大限度使用該資

     asset in its highest and best use.                                       用途的其他市場參與者，所產生的
                                                                              經濟效益。

     The Group uses valuation techniques that are appropriate in              本集團採納適用於不同情況且具備
     the circumstances and for which sufficient data are available            充分數據以供計量公允價值的估值
     to measure fair value, maximising the use of relevant                    方法，以盡量使用相關可觀察輸入
     observable inputs and minimising the use of unobservable                 數據及盡量減少使用不可觀察輸入
     inputs.                                                                  數據。

     All assets and liabilities for which fair value is measured              所有於財務報表計量或披露的資產
     or disclosed in the financial statements are categorised                 及負債乃基於對公允價值計量整體
     within the fair value hierarchy, described as follows, based             而言屬重大的最低層輸入數據按以
     on the lowest level input that is significant to the fair value          下公允價值等級分類：
     measurement as a whole:


     Level 1 – based on quoted prices (unadjusted) in active                  第一級 － 基 於 相 同 資 產 或 負 債 於
               markets for identical assets or liabilities                          活躍市場的報價（未經調
                                                                                    整）

               lowest level input that is significant to the fair                   屬重大的可觀察（直接或
               value measurement is observable, either directly or                  間接）最低層輸入數據的
               indirectly                                                           估值方法

               measurement is unobservable                                          輸入數據的估值方法

           Impairment of non-financial assets                                非金融資產減值
           Where an indication of impairment exists, or when annual          當資產（存貨、合約資產、遞延稅項
           impairment testing for an asset is required (other than           資產、金融資產、投資物業及分類
           inventories, contract assets, deferred tax assets, financial      為持作出售的非流動資產 ╱ 出售集
           assets, investment properties and non-current assets/             團除外）有減值跡象或須對資產進行
           a disposal group classified as held for sale), the asset’s        年度減值測試時，會估計資產的可
           recoverable amount is estimated. An asset’s recoverable           收回金額。資產可收回金額指資產
           amount is the higher of the asset’s or cash-generating unit’s     或現金產生單位的使用價值與其已
           value in use and its fair value less costs of disposal, and is    扣除銷售成本的公允價值兩者之中
           determined for an individual asset, unless the asset does not     的較高者，基於個別資產釐定，除
           generate cash inflows that are largely independent of those       非該資產所產生的現金流基本上獨
           from other assets or groups of assets, in which case the          立於其他資產或資產組別的現金流
           recoverable amount is determined for the cash-generating          入，在該情況下可收回金額按資產
           unit to which the asset belongs. In testing a cash-generating     所屬現金產生單位釐定。對現金產
           unit for impairment, a portion of the carrying amount of a        生單位進行減值測試時，如可合理
           corporate asset (e.g., a headquarters building) is allocated      及貫徹進行分配，則公司資產（例如
           to an individual cash-generating unit if it can be allocated      總部大廈）的一部分賬面值分配至個
           on a reasonable and consistent basis or, otherwise, to the        別現金產生單位，否則分配至最小
           smallest group of cash-generating units.                          的現金產生單位組別。

           An impairment loss is recognised only if the carrying amount      僅當資產的賬面值超逾其可收回金
           of an asset exceeds its recoverable amount. In assessing          額時方會確認減值虧損。評估使用
           value in use, the estimated future cash flows are discounted      價值時，估計未來現金流量以反映
           to their present value using a pre-tax discount rate that         當前市場評定的貨幣時間價值以及
           reflects current market assessments of the time value of          該項資產特有風險的稅前折現率折
           money and the risks specific to the asset. An impairment          現至現值。減值虧損於產生期間在
           loss is charged to the statement of profit or loss in the         損益表中列入開支，惟倘資產按重
           period in which it arises, unless the asset is carried at a       估金額列賬，則減值虧損根據該重
           revalued amount, in which case the impairment loss is             估資產的有關會計政策入賬。
           accounted for in accordance with the relevant accounting
           policy for that revalued asset.

3.   SUMMARY OF SIGNIFICANT                                            3. 重大會計政策概要（續）
     ACCOUNTING POLICIES (continued)
     Impairment of financial assets                                        金融資產減值
     The Group recognises an allowance for expected credit                 本集團就所有並非透過損益按公允
     losses (“ECLs”) for all debt instruments not held at fair value       價值列賬持有的債務工具確認預期
     through profit or loss. ECLs are based on the difference              信貸虧損（「預期信貸虧損」）撥備。
     between the contractual cash flows due in accordance with             預期信貸虧損基於根據合約到期的
     the contract and all the cash flows that the Group expects            合約現金流量與本集團預期收取的
     to receive, discounted at an approximation of the original            所有現金流量（按原實際利率的約數
     effective interest rate. The expected cash flows will include         折現）之間的差額計算。預期現金流
     cash flows from the sale of collateral held or other credit           量將包括來自出售所持有屬於合約
     enhancements that are integral to the contractual terms.              條款整體部分的抵押品或其他信貸
                                                                           加強措施的現金流量。

     General approach                                                      一般方法
     ECLs are recognised in two stages. For credit exposures               預期信貸虧損分兩個階段確認。對
     for which there has not been a significant increase in credit         於自首次確認以來信貸風險並無大
     risk since initial recognition, ECLs are provided for credit          幅增加的信貸敞口而言，就因未來
     losses that result from default events that are possible within       12 個月內可能發生的違約事件而引
     the next 12 months (a 12-month ECL). For those credit                 致的信貸虧損（一項 12 個月預期信
     exposures for which there has been a significant increase             貸虧損）計提預期信貸虧損撥備。對
     in credit risk since initial recognition, a loss allowance is         於自首次確認以來信貸風險已大幅
     required for credit losses expected over the remaining life           增加的信貸敞口而言，則須就預期
     of the exposure, irrespective of the timing of the default (a         於敞口餘下年期內的信貸虧損計提
     lifetime ECL).                                                        虧損撥備，而不論違約時間（一項存
                                                                           續期預期信貸虧損）。

     At each reporting date, the Group assesses whether                    於各報告日期，本集團評估金融工
     the credit risk on a financial instrument has increased               具的信貸風險自首次確認以來有否
     significantly since initial recognition. When making the              大幅增加。在作出該項評估時，本
     assessment, the Group compares the risk of a default                  集團將金融工具於報告日期發生違
     occurring on the financial instrument as at the reporting             約的風險與金融工具於首次確認日
     date with the risk of a default occurring on the financial            期發生違約的風險作出比較，並考
     instrument as at the date of initial recognition and considers        慮在毋須付出不必要成本或努力下
     reasonable and supportable information that is available              可得的合理及有根據資料，包括過
     without undue cost or effort, including historical and                往及前瞻性資料。當合約付款逾期
     forward-looking information. The Group considers that                 超過90 日時，本集團認為信貸風險
     there has been a significant increase in credit risk when             已大幅增加。
     contractual payments are more than 90 days past due.

           For the purpose of the consolidated statement of cash              就合併現金流量表而言，現金及現
           flows, cash and cash equivalents comprise cash on                  金等值物包括所持現金與活期存

           investments that are readily convertible into known amounts        金、價值變動風險不大且購買時一
           of cash, are subject to an insignificant risk of changes in        般不超過三個月短暫有效期的短期
           value, and have a short maturity of generally within three         高流通投資，再扣除於要求時償還
           months when acquired, less bank overdrafts which are               且為本集團現金管理所包含的銀行
           repayable on demand and form an integral part of the               透支。
           Group’s cash management.


           For the purpose of the consolidated statement of financial         合併財務狀況表的現金及現金等值

           and at banks, including term deposits, and assets similar in       現金（包括定期存款），以及性質類
           nature to cash, which are not restricted as to use.                似於現金的資產。

           Provisions                                                         撥備
           A provision is recognised when a present obligation (legal or      倘因過往事件須承擔現時責任（法定
           constructive) has arisen as a result of a past event and it is     或推定），而履行該責任可能導致未
           probable that a future outflow of resources will be required       來資源外流，且該責任所涉金額能
           to settle the obligation, provided that a reliable estimate can    夠可靠估計，則確認撥備。
           be made of the amount of the obligation.


           When the effect of discounting is material, the amount             倘折現影響重大，則確認撥備的金
           recognised for a provision is the present value at the end         額為預期履行責任所需未來開支於
           of the reporting period of the future expenditures expected        報告期末的現值。折現現值隨時間
           to be required to settle the obligation. The increase in the       增加的金額計入損益表中的財務成
           discounted present value amount arising from the passage           本內。
           of time is included in finance costs in the statement of profit
           or loss.

           Contract costs                                                   合約成本
           Other than the costs which are capitalised as inventories,       除資本化為存貨、物業、廠房及設
           property, plant and equipment and intangible assets, costs       備及無形資產的成本外，倘符合以
           incurred to fulfil a contract with a customer are capitalised    下所有條件，則為履行客戶合約而
           as an asset if all of the following criteria are met:            產生的成本資本化為資產：

                 that will be used in satisfying (or in continuing to             用以滿足（或持續滿足）履約責
                 satisfy) performance obligations in the future.                  任的資源。

           The capitalised contract costs are amortized and charged to      資本化合約成本按與向客戶轉移資
           the statement of profit or loss on a systematic basis that is    產相關商品或服務一致的基準有系
           consistent with the transfer to the customer of the goods or     統地攤銷及自損益表扣除。其他合
           services to which the asset relates. Other contract costs are    約成本於產生時支銷。
           expensed as incurred.


           Other employee benefits                                          其他僱員福利
           Pension schemes                                                  退休金計劃
           Pursuant to the relevant regulations of the PRC government,      根據中國政府有關法規，本集團旗

           (vii) Leases – Estimating the incremental borrowing                (vii) 租賃 － 估計增量借款利率
                rate
                The Group cannot readily determine the interest rate            本集團無法輕易地釐定租賃內
                implicit in a lease, and therefore, it uses an incremental      含的利率，因此，其使用增量
                borrowing rate (“IBR”) to measure lease liabilities. The        借 款 利 率（「 增 量 借 款 利 率 」）
                IBR is the rate of interest that the Group would have           來計量租賃負債。增量借款利
                to pay to borrow over a similar term, and with a similar        率是本集團於類似經濟環境中
                security, the funds necessary to obtain an asset of             為獲取與使用權資產價值相若
                a similar value to the right-of-use asset in a similar          的資產，而以類似抵押品與類
                economic environment. The IBR therefore reflects                似期限借入所需資金而需支付
                what the Group “would have to pay”, which requires              的利息比率。因此，增量借款
                estimation when no observable rates are available               利率反映本集團「需支付」的利
                (such as for subsidiaries that do not enter into                率，當無可觀察的利率時（例
                financing transactions) or when it needs to be adjusted         如就並無訂立融資交易的附屬
                to reflect the terms and conditions of the lease (for           公司而言）或當需對利率作出
                example, when leases are not in the subsidiary’s                調整以反映租賃的條款及條件
                functional currency). The Group estimates the IBR               （例如租賃並非按附屬公司的
                using observable inputs (such as market interest rates)         功能貨幣計值）時，則須作出
                when available and is required to make certain entity-          利率估計。當可觀察輸入數據
                specific estimates (such as the subsidiary’s stand-             （例如市場利率）可用時，本集
                alone credit rating).                                           團使用有關輸入數據來估計增
                                                                                量借款利率，並須作出若干實
                                                                                體特定的估計（例如附屬公司
                                                                                單獨的信貸評級）。

           (viii) Provision for completed properties held for sale            (viii) 持作出售的落成物業及發展中

                selling price and future cost to be incurred of the              生的成本。如日後的實際結果
                properties. Where the actual outcome or expectation              或預期有別於原有的估計，該

      10. INCOME TAX                                                        10. 所得稅
           Provision for PRC corporate income tax has been provided           截至 2022 年 12 月 31 日止年度，中
           at the applicable income tax rate of 25% for the year ended        國企業所得稅撥備按本集團於中國
           31 December 2022 (31 December 2021: 25%) on the                    內地的附屬公司的應評稅溢利以
           assessable profits of the Group’s subsidiaries in Mainland         25%（2021 年12 月31 日：25%）的
           China.                                                             適用所得稅率作出。

           No provision for Hong Kong profits tax has been made in            由於兩個年度均無源自香港的應評
           the financial statements as no assessable profit was derived       稅溢利，故並無就香港利得稅在財
           from Hong Kong during the years.                                   務報表中作出撥備。

           According to the requirements of the Provisional Regulations       根據 1994 年 1 月 1 日起生效的中華
           of the PRC on Land Appreciation Tax (the “LAT”) effective          人民共和國土地增值稅（「土地增值
           from 1 January 1994, and the Detailed Implementation Rules         稅」）暫行條例及1995年1月27日起
           on the Provisional Regulations of the PRC on LAT effective         生效的中華人民共和國土地增值稅
           from 27 January 1995, all income from the sale or transfer         暫行條例實施細則的規定，出售或
           of state-owned leasehold interests on land, buildings and          轉讓中國內地國有土地、建築物及
           their attached facilities in Mainland China is subject to          其附着物租賃權益所得全部收益均
           LAT at progressive rates ranging from 30% to 60% of the            須按土地增值30%至60%的累進稅
           appreciation value, with an exemption provided for property        率繳納土地增值稅，惟就普通住宅
           sales of ordinary residential properties if their appreciation     物業而言，倘增值不超過全部可扣
           values do not exceed 20% of the sum of the total deductible        稅項目總和的20%，則其物業銷售
           items.                                                             可豁免繳納土地增值稅。

           The Group has estimated, made and included in tax                  本集團已根據相關中國稅務法例及
           provision for LAT according to the requirements set forth          法規所載規定估計、計提及計入土
           in the relevant PRC tax laws and regulations. The actual           地增值稅撥備。實際的土地增值稅
           LAT liabilities are subject to the determination by the tax        負債須待物業發展項目落成後由稅
           authorities upon completion of the property development            務機關釐定，而稅務機關可能不同
           projects and the tax authorities might disagree with the           意本集團計算土地增值稅撥備的基
           basis on which the provision for LAT is calculated.                準。

           Under the income method, fair value is estimated using                  根據收入法，公允價值乃採用有關
           assumptions regarding the benefits and liabilities of                   資產壽命內所有權收益及負債的假
           ownership over the asset’s life including an exit or terminal           設進行估計（包括退出價值或終端價
           value. This method involves the projection of a series of               值）。該方法涉及對物業權益的一連

           rate is applied to the projected cash flow in order to                  現率適用於預測現金流量以便確立

           with the asset.

   The duration of the cash flows and the specific timing of              現金流量的持續時間及流入額和流
   inflows and outflows are determined by events such as rent             出額的具體時間乃由諸如租金檢
   reviews, lease renewal and related reletting, redevelopment            討、租約續簽及相關續租、重建或
   or refurbishment. The appropriate duration is driven by                翻新等事件決定。適當的持續時間
   market behaviour that is a characteristic of the class of              受 市 場 行 為（ 為 物 業 類 別 的 一 個 特
   property. The periodic cash flow is estimated as income                性 ）影 響 。 定 期 現 金 流 量 按 收 入 扣
   less non-recoverable expenses, collection losses, lease                除不可回收費用、收賬損失、租賃
   incentives, maintenance costs, agent and commission costs              獎勵、維修費用、代理和佣金費用
   and other operating and management expenses. The series                及其他經營和管理費用估算。該一
   of periodic net operating income, along with an estimate of            連串定期經營收入淨額，連同預計
   the terminal value anticipated at the end of the projection            於預測期終結時之終端價值估計金
   period, is then discounted.                                            額，折現至現值。

   A significant increase (decrease) in the market monthly rental        市場每月租金每年單獨大幅增加（減
   in isolation would result in a significant increase (decrease)        少）將導致投資物業的公允價值大幅
   in the fair value of the investment properties. A significant         增加（減少）。折現率單獨大幅增加
   increase (decrease) in the discount rate in isolation would           （減少）將導致投資物業的公允價值
   result in a significant decrease (increase) in the fair value of      大幅減少（增加）。
   the investment properties.

   The provision rates are based on ageing for groupings of             貸虧損。撥備率根據具有類似虧損

   by customer type). The calculation reflects the probability-         型劃分）的賬齡而釐定。該計算反映

   Under the market method, fair value is estimated based on               根據市場法，公允價值乃根據市場
   the market-determined prices adjusted with reference to                 釐定價格釐定，其按照物種、年
   the species, age, growing condition and cost incurred to                齡、成長情況及已產生成本作出調
   reflect differences in characteristics and/or stages of growth          整，以反映生物資產在成長特徵
   of biological assets less transaction costs to sell. Under the          及 ╱ 或階段方面的差異，並減去出
   income method, fair value is estimated using assumptions                售的交易成本。根據收入法，公允
   regarding the benefits and liabilities of ownership over                價值乃採用有關資產壽命內所有權
   the asset’s life including an exit or terminal value. This              收益及負債的假設進行估計（包括退
   method involves the projection of a series of cash flows on             出價值或終端價值）。該方法涉及對
   a biological asset interest. A market-derived discount rate             生物資產權益的一連串現金流量的
   is applied to the projected cash flow in order to establish             預測。市場衍生的折現率適用於預
   the present value of the income stream associated with the              測現金流量以便確立與資產有關的
   asset.                                                                  收入流的現值。

32. RESERVES                                                        32. 儲備
   The amounts of the Group’s reserves and the movements                本集團的儲備金額及其變動已載於
   therein are presented in the consolidated statement of               合併權益變動表。
   changes in equity.


   (a) Capital reserve                                                  (a) 資本儲備
        The amounts represent deemed contributions                          該款項指視為權益持有人注資
        from equity holders less deemed distributions to                    減視為分派予權益持有人、本
        equity holders, considerations paid by the Group to                 集團向非控股權益持有人就其
        non-controlling equity holders over their then share of             當時分佔所收購附屬公司資產
        net assets of subsidiaries acquired, and dilution upon              淨值支付代價及非控股權益持
        capital contributions by the non-controlling equity                 有人注資時的攤薄。
        holders.


   (b) Statutory reserves                                               (b) 法定儲備
        In accordance with the PRC Company Law and                          根據中國公司法及中國附屬公
        the PRC subsidiaries’ articles of association, each                 司的章程細則，在中國註冊為
        subsidiary registered in the PRC as a domestic                      內資公司的附屬公司各自須撥
        company is required to appropriate 10% of its annual                出 10% 的年度法定純利（經抵
        statutory net profit as determined under generally                  銷任何往年虧損後）至法定盈
        accepted accounting principles of The People’s                      餘儲備。年度法定純利按中華
        Republic of China (“PRC GAAP”) (after offsetting any                人民共和國公認會計準則（「中
        prior years’ losses) to the statutory surplus reserve.              國會計準則」）釐定。倘該儲備
        When the balance of this reserve fund reaches 50%                   金結餘達該實體資本的50%，
        of the entity’s capital, any further appropriation is               則可選擇作出任何額外撥款。
        optional. The statutory reserve can be utilised to offset           法定儲備可用作抵銷往年虧損
        prior years’ losses or to increase capital. However,                或增資。然而，作上述用途
        the balance of the statutory surplus reserve must be                後，該法定盈餘儲備結餘須維
        maintained at a minimum of 25% of the capital after                 持為不少於資本的25%。
        these usages.

   arising from the overdue payments of construction costs              期建築成本款項產生的利息約人民
   of approximately RMB977,000. As at 31 December 2022,                 幣 977,000 元。於 2022 年 12 月 31
   certain bank accounts of the Group of RMB18,938,000                  日，本集團若干銀行賬戶涉款人民

   property preservation. As of the date of approval of the             民法院凍結作財產保全。於財務報
   financial statements, the trials of the cases are yet to be          表批准日期，案件尚未排期審訊。
   scheduled. The Group has made full provision for payments            基於現有證據及已聽取法律意見，
   of construction costs due to the possibility of the Group            考慮到本集團須承擔付款責任的可

40. FAIR VALUE AND FAIR VALUE HIERARCHY                             40. 金 融 工 具 公 允 價 值 及 公 允
    OF FINANCIAL INSTRUMENTS (continued)                                價值等級（續）
   The Group’s finance department headed by the finance                 以本集團財務經理為首的財務部門
   manager is responsible for determining the policies and              負責釐定金融工具公允價值計量的
   procedures for the fair value measurement of financial               政策及程序。財務經理直接向財務
   instruments. The finance manager reports directly to                 總監及董事會報告。於各報告日
   the chief financial officer and the board of directors. At           期，財務部門分析金融工具的價值
   each reporting date, the finance department analyses                 變動並釐定估值中適用的主要參
   the movements in the values of financial instruments and             數。估值由財務總監審核及批准。
   determines the major inputs applied in the valuation. The            估值過程及結果與董事會每年討論
   valuation is reviewed and approved by the chief financial            一次以進行年度財務申報。
   officer. The valuation process and results are discussed
   with the board of directors once a year for annual financial
   reporting.


   The fair values of the financial assets and liabilities are          計入金融資產及金融負債的公允價
   included at the amount at which the instrument could be              值，乃自願訂約方於目前交易（受脅
   exchanged in a current transaction between willing parties,          迫或清盤出售交易除外）中可交換工
   other than in a forced or liquidation sale. The following            具的金額。估計公允價值時使用的
   methods and assumptions were used to estimate the fair               方法及假設如下：
   values:


   The fair values of the interest-bearing bank and other               按固定利率計息的計息銀行及其他
   borrowings with fixed interest rates have been calculated            借款的公允價值按適用於具有類似
   by discounting the expected future cash flows using rates            條款、信貸風險及剩餘年期的工具
   currently available for instruments with similar terms, credit       的現行利率折現預期未來現金流量
   risk and remaining maturities. The changes in fair value             計算。本集團本身於 2022 年 12 月
   as a result of the Group’s own non-performance risk for              31 日的計息銀行及其他借款的不履
   the interest-bearing bank and other borrowings as at 31              約風險以致的公允價值變動被評定
   December 2022 were assessed to be insignificant.                     為不重大。

      40. FAIR VALUE AND FAIR VALUE HIERARCHY                               40. 金 融 工 具 公 允 價 值 及 公 允
          OF FINANCIAL INSTRUMENTS (continued)                                  價值等級（續）
           The fair values of unlisted equity investments designated           指定為透過其他全面收益按公允價
           at fair value through other comprehensive income have               值列賬的非上市股本投資的公允價
           been estimated using a market-based valuation technique             值，乃採用市場估值方法基於並無
           based on assumptions that are not supported by observable           可觀察市場價格或比率支持的假
           market prices or rates. The valuation requires the directors        設估計得出。進行估值時，董事
           to determine comparable public companies (peers) based              須根據行業、規模、槓桿及策略
           on industry, size, leverage and strategy, and to calculate          決 定 可 比 較 公 眾 公 司（ 同 業 ）， 以
           an appropriate price multiple, such as enterprise value             及就每間已識別的可比較公司計算
           to earnings before interest, taxes, depreciation and                相關價格倍數，例如企業價值相
           amortisation (“EV/EBITDA”) multiple and price to earnings           對未計利息、稅項、折舊及攤銷前
           (“P/E”) multiple, for each comparable company identified.           盈利（「E V / E B I T D A」）倍數及市盈
           The multiple is calculated by dividing the enterprise value        （「P/E」）率。該倍數乃按可比較公
           of the comparable company by an earnings measure.                   司的企業價值除以盈利的計量方法
           The trading multiple is then discounted for considerations          計算得出。交易倍數因而就可比較
           such as illiquidity and size differences between the                公司基於公司特定事實及情況的流
           comparable companies based on company-specific facts                動性不足及規模差異等考慮因素作
           and circumstances. The discounted multiple is applied to            出折讓。折讓後的倍數應用於非上
           the corresponding earnings measure of the unlisted equity           市股本投資的相關盈利計量，以計
           investments to measure the fair value. The directors believe        算公允價值。董事相信，因估值方
           that the estimated fair values resulting from the valuation         法產生的估計公允價值（於合併財務
           technique, which are recorded in the consolidated statement         狀況表記賬）及相關的公允價值變動
           of financial position, and the related changes in fair values,     （於其他全面收益記賬）為合理，且
           which are recorded in other comprehensive income, are               於報告期末為最適當的價值。
           reasonable, and that they were the most appropriate values
           at the end of the reporting period.


           The Group invests in an unlisted unit trust fund investment         本集團投資於透過損益按公允價值
           at fair value through profit or loss. The Group has estimated       列賬的非上市單位信託投資。本集
           the fair value of this unlisted investment by using a               團已透過使用貼現現金流量估值模
           discounted cash flow valuation model based on the market            式，根據具有類似條款及風險的工
           interest rates of instruments with similar terms and risks.         具的市場利率估計此項非上市投資
                                                                               的公允價值。

           The Group invests in the equity of a company listed on              本集團投資於一間在中國內地企業
           the over-the-counter exchange for companies in Mainland             場外交易所掛牌的公司的股權。本
           China. The Group has estimated the fair value of the debt           集團已透過使用二項樹模式估計該
           instrument by using the Binomial Tree Model.                        債務工具的公允價值。

41. F I N A N C I A L R I S K M A N A G E M E N T                    41. 財務風險管理目標及政策
    OBJECTIVES AND POLICIES
    The Group’s principal financial instruments comprise                 本集團的主要金融工具包括計息銀
    interest-bearing bank and other borrowings, amounts due              行及其他借款、應收 ╱ 應付關聯公
    from/to related companies, financial assets at fair value            司款項、透過損益按公允價值列賬
    through profit or loss, pledged deposits and cash and bank           的金融資產、已抵押存款以及現金
    balances. The main purpose of these financial instruments is         及銀行結餘。此等金融工具主要用
    to raise finance for the Group’s operations. The Group has           於為本集團籌集經營資金。本集團
    various other financial assets and liabilities such as trade         有直接來自其運營的多種其他金融
    receivables, trade payables, other receivables and other             資產及負債，例如貿易應收款項、
    payables, which arise directly from its operations.                  貿易應付款項、其他應收款項及其
                                                                         他應付款項。


    It is, and has been throughout the year under review, the            本集團的政策為及於回顧年度一直
    Group’s policy that no trading in financial instruments shall        為不進行金融工具交易。
    be undertaken.


    The main risks arising from the Group’s financial instruments        本集團的金融工具產生的主要風險
    are interest rate risk, foreign currency risk, credit risk and       為利率風險、外匯風險、信貸風險
    liquidity risk. The board of directors reviews and agrees            及流動資金風險。董事會檢討及協
    policies for managing each of these risks and they are               議控制各類風險的政策，有關政策
    summarised below.                                                    概述於下文。

    OBJECTIVES AND POLICIES (continued)                                 （續）
    Capital management                                                   資本管理
    The primary objectives of the Group’s capital management             本集團資本管理的首要目標是維護
    are to safeguard the Group’s ability to continue as a going          本集團的持續經營能力及維持穩健
    concern and to maintain healthy capital ratios in order to           的資本化比率，以支持其業務及為
    support its business and maximise equity holders’ value.             權益持有人帶來最大價值。

    The Group manages its capital structure and makes                    本集團根據經濟狀況變動及相關資
    adjustments to it in light of changes in economic conditions         產的風險特點管理及調整資本結
    and the risk characteristics of the underlying assets. To            構。為維持或調整資本結構，本集
    maintain or adjust the capital structure, the Group may              團或會調整派付予股東的股息、向
    adjust the dividend payment to shareholders, return capital          股東歸還資本或發行新股。本集
    to shareholders or issue new shares. The Group is not                團不受任何外部施加的資本要求
    subject to any externally imposed capital requirements. No           規限。於截至 2022 年 12 月 31 日及
    changes were made in the objectives, policies or processes           2021 年 12 月 31 日止年度，目標、
    during the years ended 31 December 2022 and 31                       政策或程序並無任何變動。
    December 2021.


    The Group monitors capital using a net debt to total                 本集團利用淨負債總權益比率來監
    equity ratio. Net debt includes interest-bearing bank and            察資本。債務淨額包括計息銀行及
    other borrowings and lease liabilities, less cash and cash           其他借貸以及租賃負債，減現金及
    equivalents. The gearing ratios as at the end of the reporting       現金等值物。於報告期末的負債比
    periods were as follows:                                             率如下：
